    "36f50259-29a6-4ea1-979a-b273d873eade": {
      "id": "36f50259-29a6-4ea1-979a-b273d873eade",
      "message": {
        "id": "36f50259-29a6-4ea1-979a-b273d873eade",

          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757420889.277667,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "人文字の航空写真は、計画次第でかなり綺麗に仕上がります。コツは「精密な下準備」と「現場での統率」にあります。小学校イベントで実施するなら、以下の手順がおすすめです。\n\n---\n\n## **1. 文字デザインの決定**\n- **太めでシンプルなフォント**を選ぶのがコツ  \n  → 明朝体よりゴシック体。筆画が細いと崩れやすいです。\n- 一文字あたりの縦横比を意識  \n  → 航空写真では横長に写りやすいので、実際より少し縦長にデザインすると綺麗に映ります。\n\n---\n\n## **2. グラウンドに原寸大の下絵を作る**\n### 方法A：紙ベースでの割り付け（おすすめ）\n1. 校庭の実測寸法を測る（例：縦60m×横40m）。\n2. 校庭の縮尺図を作り、A4紙で方眼紙を用意。\n3. 方眼紙に描いた文字を分割して、現場では1マス=1mなどのスケールで再現。\n4. 粉チョーク、テープ、ビニールひもなどで校庭に線を引く。\n\n### 方法B：デジタルでのレイアウト\n- Google Earth やドローンマップで航空写真を取り込み、実寸サイズで文字を重ねる。\n- これをプリントアウトして当日の配置図にする。\n\n---\n\n## **3. 人の配置計画**\n- **1文字ごとに必要人数を計算**  \n  → 例：横10m×縦8mの文字なら、1m²に1人配置で80人必要。\n- 学年やクラスごとに文字を割り当てると統率しやすいです。\n- 背丈を意識して並べる  \n  → 低学年は内側、高学年は外側にすると輪郭が綺麗に出ます。\n\n---\n\n## **4. 当日の統率方法**\n- **上から見た配置図を事前に配布**  \n  → 児童にもわかるように、色分けしたマップを作るとスムーズ。\n- グラウンドに「番号付きスポット」を貼る  \n  → 例：ガムテープに数字を書き、子どもたちはその番号に立つだけ。\n- メガホン・トランシーバーを使って誘導  \n  → 体育教師など経験者をリーダーに立てると混乱が少ないです。\n\n---\n\n## **5. 写真撮影の工夫**\n- **ドローン撮影**が理想。高度30m〜50m程度で垂直撮影。\n- 人文字全体が入るようにフレームを先に決めておく。\n- 光の向きにも注意。逆光だと顔がつぶれます。\n\n---\n\n## **6. 成功のポイント**\n- 本番前に「試し並び」を1回やっておく。\n- 文字のバランスが崩れやすいので、上空からドローンでチェックしながら微調整。\n- 「整列担当」「安全管理担当」「撮影担当」を明確に分けると混乱が減ります。\n\n---\n\nもし希望があれば、実寸サイズの校庭図をもとにした **人文字レイアウト図**（1m単位・人数割り付き）を作ることもできます。  \nたとえば「ありがとう」など具体的な文字が決まっていれば、必要人数・配置計画・下絵PDFまで作成可能です。  \n\n次は、やりますか？"
          ]
        },

          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "「走　150」の文字で作成お願いします"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": null,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "dictation": false,
          "request_id": "97c6b14ec81a314c-KIX",
          "message_source": null,
          "timestamp_": "absolute"
        },
        "recipient": "all",
        "channel": null
      },
      "parent": "36f50259-29a6-4ea1-979a-b273d873eade",
      "children": [
        "1e2e8c02-f117-4f0f-80f3-df28d3bfc597"
      ]
    },
    "1e2e8c02-f117-4f0f-80f3-df28d3bfc597": {
      "id": "1e2e8c02-f117-4f0f-80f3-df28d3bfc597",
      "message": {
        "id": "1e2e8c02-f117-4f0f-80f3-df28d3bfc597",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757420981.867394,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "了解しました！  \n「走 150」の人文字レイアウトを、小学校グラウンドで使える形で設計します。  \nここでは **必要人数の試算・文字サイズ・配置図** まで具体的に作ります。  \n最終的には **1m単位での人数割り付き配置図（PDF）** も作れますが、まずは計画の全体像を整理します。\n\n---\n\n## **1. 前提条件（仮定値）**\n- 校庭サイズ（例）：**縦60m × 横40m**\n- 人の立ち間隔：**1mピッチ**\n- 対象人数：全校児童＋教職員 約 **200人** 想定  \n  （実人数がわかれば最適化します）\n\n---\n\n## **2. 文字デザインとサイズ**\n### 「走」1文字\n- 推奨サイズ：**縦15m × 横15m**  \n- 太めのゴシック体に近い形状で線幅 **1.5m**  \n- 必要人数：約 **80人**\n\n### 「150」（数字3文字）\n- 推奨サイズ：**縦12m × 横7m × 3文字**  \n- 各数字の線幅 **1.2m**  \n- 必要人数：約 **35人 × 3 = 105人**\n\n**合計必要人数：約185人**  \n→ 200人程度いれば十分対応可能。\n\n---\n\n## **3. 配置のイメージ**\n```\n  ← 横方向40m →\n┌───────────────────────┐\n│                                                             │\n│       [   走   ]      [   1   ]  [   5   ]  [   0   ]       │\n│                                                             │\n│   （縦15m×横15m）   （各縦12m×横7m）               │\n│                                                             │\n└───────────────────────┘\n   ↑\n 縦60m\n```\n- 「走」は左側に大きめに配置\n- 「150」は右側に横並びでバランスを取る\n- 校庭中央を基準に配置するとドローン撮影時に綺麗に映ります\n\n---\n\n## **4. 当日の準備方法**\n### 校庭への下絵作成\n1. 校庭を1m間隔でメジャー＋ピンで測量\n2. 文字の角を基準にビニール紐で囲む\n3. 線の内側に粉チョークで輪郭を描く\n4. 子どもたちの立ち位置には **番号付きテープ** を貼る  \n   → 例：「走-23」「1-5」など\n\n---\n\n## **5. ドローン撮影の推奨設定**\n- **高度**：35m〜50m\n- **カメラ角度**：真下（垂直）\n- **時間帯**：午前10時〜11時頃が影が短くおすすめ\n\n---\n\n## **6. 次のステップ**\nもし人数とグラウンドの正確なサイズがわかれば、**1m単位のレイアウト図**を作成して、  \n各児童に割り当て番号まで付けたPDFを作れます。  \nこれがあれば先生方の誘導が劇的に楽になります。\n\n---\n\nこのあと、  \n- 校庭の正確な **縦横サイズ**  \n- 児童と大人の **総人数**  \n- 「走150」を入れたい **レイアウト方向**（縦書きか横書きか）\n\nこの3つを教えていただければ、  \n次は「1m方眼紙上に配置した本番用レイアウト図（PDF）」を作ります。\n\nどうしますか？  \n人数と校庭サイズ、わかりますか？"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": true,

    "af3c440e-ab4d-48a9-9a10-137efe940942": {
      "id": "af3c440e-ab4d-48a9-9a10-137efe940942",

            "工程のサイズ\n横20m 縦10m\n\n児童　100名\n大人　50名"

        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": null,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "dictation": false,
          "request_id": "97c6b38e8a7f314c-KIX",

          "timestamp_": "absolute"
        },

      "parent": "1e2e8c02-f117-4f0f-80f3-df28d3bfc597",
      "children": [
        "5b4818c7-e15d-4881-b0d5-2f65648b121e"
      ]
    },
    "5b4818c7-e15d-4881-b0d5-2f65648b121e": {
      "id": "5b4818c7-e15d-4881-b0d5-2f65648b121e",
      "message": {
        "id": "5b4818c7-e15d-4881-b0d5-2f65648b121e",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421073.973973,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "ありがとうございます！  \n校庭サイズが **横20m × 縦10m**、総人数が **150名（児童100名＋大人50名）**ですね。  \nこれを踏まえて、「走150」の人文字レイアウトを最適化して設計します。  \n今回は **限られたスペースと人数**でバランスよく配置することが重要です。\n\n---\n\n## **1. 前提条件**\n- グラウンド：横20m × 縦10m（横長）\n- 総人数：150名\n- 1人あたりの立ちスペース：約 **1m²**\n- 航空写真用なので **横書き**レイアウトにします。\n\n---\n\n## **2. 文字サイズの最適化**\n人数150名を4文字で分配するので、**1文字あたり約35〜40人**で設計します。  \n「走」を少し大きめにして迫力を出し、「150」は揃えてコンパクトにします。\n\n| 文字 | 縦サイズ | 横サイズ | 線幅 | 必要人数(目安) |\n|------|---------|---------|------|----------------|\n| 走   | 約4.0m  | 約4.0m  | 1.0m | 約40人 |\n| 1    | 約3.0m  | 約2.5m  | 1.0m | 約25人 |\n| 5    | 約3.0m  | 約2.5m  | 1.0m | 約30人 |\n| 0    | 約3.0m  | 約2.5m  | 1.0m | 約30人 |\n| **合計** | — | — | — | **約125人** |\n\n残りの**25人**はスペース調整や外枠補強として「走」や「0」に回すと美しくなります。\n\n---\n\n## **3. 校庭レイアウトイメージ**\n```\n   ← 横20m →\n┌────────────────────┐\n│    [ 走 ]   [ 1 ]  [ 5 ]  [ 0 ] │\n│   (4m)   (2.5m) (2.5m) (2.5m)  │\n│                                 │\n│   ─────────────────────   │\n│   前列: 低学年                │\n│   後列: 高学年＋大人          │\n└────────────────────┘\n   ↑ 縦10m ↑\n```\n- 「走」を左に大きめ配置、右に「150」を等間隔で並べる\n- 各文字間は **0.5m** 程度のスペースを確保\n- 背丈で並べると輪郭が綺麗に出ます  \n  → 低学年は内側・前列、大人は外周や文字の輪郭強調に配置\n\n---\n\n## **4. 当日の進め方**\n### 校庭での準備\n1. **基準線を引く**  \n   - 校庭中央を基準に、横20m×縦10mの枠をロープで囲む。\n2. **文字ごとに区画割り**  \n   - 紙に書いたレイアウト図を元に、地面にチョークで文字輪郭を描く。\n3. **立ち位置に番号を振る**  \n   - 例：「走-15」「1-03」「5-22」など。\n4. **児童・大人をグループごとに並べる**  \n   - 学年・背丈・文字パートで事前割り当てすると混乱が減る。\n\n---\n\n## **5. ドローン撮影のコツ**\n- **高度**：20〜30m（校庭が横長なので低め）\n- **角度**：真下\n- **時間帯**：午前10〜11時頃が影が短く理想\n- 予行演習で一度上空チェック → 崩れていたらすぐ修正\n\n---\n\n## **6. 次のステップ**\n次は **1m単位の正確な配置図（PDF）** を作成します。  \nこれには以下を盛り込みます：\n- 校庭に合わせた実寸レイアウト\n- 各文字の形状をゴシック体で再現\n- 立ち位置番号をすべて記載\n- 児童・大人の割り当てまで反映\n\n---\n\nこのPDFを先生方に配布すれば、  \n当日の誘導は「立ち位置番号を呼ぶだけ」で済むので混乱がほぼゼロになります。\n\nここで確認ですが、  \n1. 「走150」は横書きでOKですか？  \n2. ドローン撮影は予定していますか？  \n\nもしOKなら、次のステップで**1m方眼紙ベースの本番レイアウトPDF**を作ります。  \nやりますか？"
          ]
        },

    "aa40476d-03a8-48a1-b0b0-4db2842576d6": {

    "176a1de7-c097-4647-8ba5-7a9cbb37397f": {
      "id": "176a1de7-c097-4647-8ba5-7a9cbb37397f",
      "message": {
        "id": "176a1de7-c097-4647-8ba5-7a9cbb37397f",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421128.497579,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# We'll generate a 1m grid (20m x 10m) and place \"走150\" composed of thick strokes.\r\n# People (points) will be placed at ~1m spacing inside those stroke areas and numbered per glyph.\r\n# The output will be a PDF suitable for printing with all positions labeled.\r\n\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\n# Geometry helpers\r\ndef rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh):\r\n    return (x >= rx) and (x <= rx+rw) and (y >= ry) and (y <= ry+rh)\r\n\r\ndef make_points_in_rects(rects, spacing=1.0):\r\n    # Create grid of candidate points across the bounding box of all rects\r\n    if not rects:\r\n        return []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in rects)\r\n    pts = []\r\n    # Place points on integer grid (centered on meters)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            # If point lies inside any rect, include\r\n            inside = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in rects)\r\n            if inside:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Define stroke rectangles for each glyph in global coordinates\r\n# Grid: width=20m, height=10m\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters\r\ngap = 0.5  # 0.5m gap between glyphs\r\n# \"走\" box at left: width 4m, height 4m, positioned centered vertically\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 4.0, 4.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.7, (10 - zou_h)/2  # small left margin\r\n\r\n# Build blocky \"走\" using rectangles inside its 4x4 box\r\n# We'll approximate strokes with 1m thickness\r\nt = 1.0\r\n\r\nZ = []  # list of rects (x,y,w,h) in global coords for \"走\"\r\n\r\n# Structure (very simplified, blocky, readable from above):\r\n# Top horizontal bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.2, zou_y + zou_h - t - 0.2, zou_w - 0.4, t))\r\n# Middle horizontal bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.3, zou_y + 2.1, zou_w - 0.6, t))\r\n# Vertical stem center\r\nZ.append((zou_x + (zou_w - t)/2, zou_y + 0.6, t, 2.8))\r\n# Bottom base bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.5, zou_y + 0.4, zou_w - 1.0, t))\r\n# Left foot diagonal-ish (approx as vertical)\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.6, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n# Right foot diagonal-ish (approx as vertical)\r\nZ.append((zou_x + zou_w - 0.6 - t, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n\r\n# \"1\",\"5\",\"0\" each 2.5m wide, 3m tall, centered vertically\r\nnum_w, num_h = 2.5, 3.0\r\nbase_y = (10 - num_h)/2\r\n\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\n# Build \"1\"\r\nO = []\r\n# main vertical\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - t/2, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# small base\r\nO.append((one_x + 0.3, base_y, num_w - 0.6, t))\r\n# top serif\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - 0.7, base_y + num_h - t, 1.4, t))\r\n\r\n# Build \"5\"\r\nF = []\r\n# top bar\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\n# upper vertical left\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2, t, num_h/2))\r\n# middle bar\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2 - t/2, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n# lower curve approximated by vertical right + bottom bar\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w*0.2, base_y, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n\r\n# Build \"0\" as ring (outer minus inner). We'll approximate with thick ring as four rectangles.\r\nZ0_outer = []\r\n# outer rectangle frame segments\r\n# Left\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# Right\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# Top\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\n# Bottom\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, num_w, t))\r\n\r\n# Inner hole: we'll subtract by simply not placing points inside inner rect\r\nzero_inner_margin = 0.6  # hole margin\r\nzero_inner = (zero_x + zero_inner_margin, base_y + zero_inner_margin,\r\n              num_w - 2*zero_inner_margin, num_h - 2*zero_inner_margin)\r\n\r\ndef points_for_ring(outer_rects, inner_rect, spacing=1.0):\r\n    # Collect points inside any outer rect but outside inner rect\r\n    pts = []\r\n    # Determine rough bounds\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            in_outer = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in outer_rects)\r\n            in_inner = rect_contains(x, y, *inner_rect)\r\n            if in_outer and not in_inner:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Generate points\r\npts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=1.0)\r\n\r\n# Count and, if exceeding total budget (150), trim evenly across glyphs\r\ntotal_points = len(pts_Z) + len(pts_O) + len(pts_F) + len(pts_0)\r\n\r\npts_Z, pts_O, pts_F, pts_0, total_points\n"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",

      },
      "parent": "aa40476d-03a8-48a1-b0b0-4db2842576d6",

        "b5832ec4-db47-4a21-8763-4c28c8dcfc34"

        "content": {
          "content_type": "execution_output",
          "text": "([(1.4, 3.9),\n  (1.4, 4.9),\n  (1.4, 5.9),\n  (2.4, 3.9),\n  (2.4, 4.9),\n  (2.4, 5.9),\n  (3.4, 3.9),\n  (3.4, 4.9),\n  (3.4, 5.9)],\n [(6.0, 4.0), (6.0, 5.0), (6.0, 6.0)],\n [(8.7, 4.0), (8.7, 5.0), (8.7, 6.0), (9.7, 4.0), (9.7, 5.0), (9.7, 6.0)],\n [(11.7, 4.0), (11.7, 5.0), (11.7, 6.0), (12.7, 4.0), (12.7, 6.0)],\n 23)"
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": null,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "is_complete": true,
          "aggregate_result": {
            "status": "success",
            "run_id": "21c79ab4-5dc4-47e3-b329-dc8a363aae9a",
            "start_time": 1757421215.3930006,
            "update_time": 1757421220.125586,
            "code": "# We'll generate a 1m grid (20m x 10m) and place \"走150\" composed of thick strokes.\r\n# People (points) will be placed at ~1m spacing inside those stroke areas and numbered per glyph.\r\n# The output will be a PDF suitable for printing with all positions labeled.\r\n\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\n# Geometry helpers\r\ndef rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh):\r\n    return (x >= rx) and (x <= rx+rw) and (y >= ry) and (y <= ry+rh)\r\n\r\ndef make_points_in_rects(rects, spacing=1.0):\r\n    # Create grid of candidate points across the bounding box of all rects\r\n    if not rects:\r\n        return []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in rects)\r\n    pts = []\r\n    # Place points on integer grid (centered on meters)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            # If point lies inside any rect, include\r\n            inside = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in rects)\r\n            if inside:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Define stroke rectangles for each glyph in global coordinates\r\n# Grid: width=20m, height=10m\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters\r\ngap = 0.5  # 0.5m gap between glyphs\r\n# \"走\" box at left: width 4m, height 4m, positioned centered vertically\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 4.0, 4.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.7, (10 - zou_h)/2  # small left margin\r\n\r\n# Build blocky \"走\" using rectangles inside its 4x4 box\r\n# We'll approximate strokes with 1m thickness\r\nt = 1.0\r\n\r\nZ = []  # list of rects (x,y,w,h) in global coords for \"走\"\r\n\r\n# Structure (very simplified, blocky, readable from above):\r\n# Top horizontal bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.2, zou_y + zou_h - t - 0.2, zou_w - 0.4, t))\r\n# Middle horizontal bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.3, zou_y + 2.1, zou_w - 0.6, t))\r\n# Vertical stem center\r\nZ.append((zou_x + (zou_w - t)/2, zou_y + 0.6, t, 2.8))\r\n# Bottom base bar\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.5, zou_y + 0.4, zou_w - 1.0, t))\r\n# Left foot diagonal-ish (approx as vertical)\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.6, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n# Right foot diagonal-ish (approx as vertical)\r\nZ.append((zou_x + zou_w - 0.6 - t, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n\r\n# \"1\",\"5\",\"0\" each 2.5m wide, 3m tall, centered vertically\r\nnum_w, num_h = 2.5, 3.0\r\nbase_y = (10 - num_h)/2\r\n\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\n# Build \"1\"\r\nO = []\r\n# main vertical\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - t/2, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# small base\r\nO.append((one_x + 0.3, base_y, num_w - 0.6, t))\r\n# top serif\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - 0.7, base_y + num_h - t, 1.4, t))\r\n\r\n# Build \"5\"\r\nF = []\r\n# top bar\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\n# upper vertical left\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2, t, num_h/2))\r\n# middle bar\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2 - t/2, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n# lower curve approximated by vertical right + bottom bar\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w*0.2, base_y, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n\r\n# Build \"0\" as ring (outer minus inner). We'll approximate with thick ring as four rectangles.\r\nZ0_outer = []\r\n# outer rectangle frame segments\r\n# Left\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# Right\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h))\r\n# Top\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\n# Bottom\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, num_w, t))\r\n\r\n# Inner hole: we'll subtract by simply not placing points inside inner rect\r\nzero_inner_margin = 0.6  # hole margin\r\nzero_inner = (zero_x + zero_inner_margin, base_y + zero_inner_margin,\r\n              num_w - 2*zero_inner_margin, num_h - 2*zero_inner_margin)\r\n\r\ndef points_for_ring(outer_rects, inner_rect, spacing=1.0):\r\n    # Collect points inside any outer rect but outside inner rect\r\n    pts = []\r\n    # Determine rough bounds\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            in_outer = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in outer_rects)\r\n            in_inner = rect_contains(x, y, *inner_rect)\r\n            if in_outer and not in_inner:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Generate points\r\npts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=1.0)\r\npts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=1.0)\r\n\r\n# Count and, if exceeding total budget (150), trim evenly across glyphs\r\ntotal_points = len(pts_Z) + len(pts_O) + len(pts_F) + len(pts_0)\r\n\r\npts_Z, pts_O, pts_F, pts_0, total_points\n",
            "end_time": 1757421220.125586,
            "final_expression_output": "([(1.4, 3.9),\n  (1.4, 4.9),\n  (1.4, 5.9),\n  (2.4, 3.9),\n  (2.4, 4.9),\n  (2.4, 5.9),\n  (3.4, 3.9),\n  (3.4, 4.9),\n  (3.4, 5.9)],\n [(6.0, 4.0), (6.0, 5.0), (6.0, 6.0)],\n [(8.7, 4.0), (8.7, 5.0), (8.7, 6.0), (9.7, 4.0), (9.7, 5.0), (9.7, 6.0)],\n [(11.7, 4.0), (11.7, 5.0), (11.7, 6.0), (12.7, 4.0), (12.7, 6.0)],\n 23)",

            "code": "# Try spacing 0.4m for denser placement\r\npts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=0.4)\r\npts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=0.4)\r\npts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=0.4)\r\npts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=0.4)\r\ntotal_points = len(pts_Z) + len(pts_O) + len(pts_F) + len(pts_0)\r\nlen(pts_Z), len(pts_O), len(pts_F), len(pts_0), total_points\n",
            "end_time": 1757421233.9105682,
            "final_expression_output": "(64, 23, 39, 22, 148)",

    "c13e5659-6c93-44b9-944e-2279f6b7c6d2": {
      "id": "c13e5659-6c93-44b9-944e-2279f6b7c6d2",
      "message": {
        "id": "c13e5659-6c93-44b9-944e-2279f6b7c6d2",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421233.91726,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "from matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\n\r\n# Assign IDs per glyph\r\ndef enumerate_points(pts, prefix):\r\n    # Sort points for consistent numbering: left-to-right, bottom-to-top\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(pts, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    labeled = [(i+1, p[0], p[1]) for i, p in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i:02d}\", x, y) for (i, x, y) in labeled]\r\n\r\nLZ = enumerate_points(pts_Z, \"走\")\r\nL1 = enumerate_points(pts_O, \"1\")\r\nL5 = enumerate_points(pts_F, \"5\")\r\nL0 = enumerate_points(pts_0, \"0\")\r\n\r\ntotal = len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)\r\n\r\npdf_path = \"/mnt/data/走150_人文字レイアウト_20x10m_配置図.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path) as pdf:\r\n    # Page 1: Overview map\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))  # A4 landscape\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, 21):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, 10], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, 11):\r\n        ax.plot([0, 20], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,20,20,0,0],[0,0,10,10,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Draw glyph stroke outlines (rectangles) for reference\r\n    for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in Z + O + F + Z0_outer:\r\n        rect = plt.Rectangle((rx, ry), rw, rh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0)\r\n        ax.add_patch(rect)\r\n    # Inner hole of 0\r\n    (irx, iry, irw, irh) = zero_inner\r\n    rect_in = plt.Rectangle((irx, iry), irw, irh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\")\r\n    ax.add_patch(rect_in)\r\n    # Plot points with small markers and labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=2)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.05, y+0.05, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    plot_group(LZ); plot_group(L1); plot_group(L5); plot_group(L0)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, 20.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, 10.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"走150 人文字レイアウト｜20m×10m｜1マス=1m｜配置密度=0.4mピッチ\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"メートル（横）\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"メートル（縦）\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: Counts and instructions\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    lines = []\r\n    lines.append(\"【人数内訳】（合計: {}名）\".format(total))\r\n    lines.append(\"・走: {}名\".format(len(LZ)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・1 : {}名\".format(len(L1)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・5 : {}名\".format(len(L5)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・0 : {}名\".format(len(L0)))\r\n    lines.append(\"\")\r\n    lines.append(\"【当日運用の手順（要約）】\")\r\n    lines.append(\"1) 校庭に外枠20m×10mをロープで作成、1m間隔でマーキング。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"2) PDFの矩形線に沿ってチョークで輪郭を描画（「0」の内側矩形は立入禁止）。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"3) 児童=『走』『1』『5』内側中心、 大人=『走』『0』外周強調に配置。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"4) 呼び出し: グループごとに「走-01〜」「1-01〜」の順で整列。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"5) ドローン高度20〜30m、真下撮影。上空確認→微調整→本番。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"\")\r\n    lines.append(\"【注意】\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・配置点は0.4mピッチ。混雑時は交互に間引き可（偶数番号を省く等）。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・安全最優先。走らない。ロープ・ペグに注意。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・強風時はドローン運用を中止し、梯子/屋上からの撮影に切替。\")\r\n    txt = \"\\n\".join(lines)\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path\n"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": false,
        "weight": 1,

          "aggregate_result": {
            "status": "success",
            "run_id": "94a10ed5-6cd9-47a2-9f45-855760a8a1b6",
            "start_time": 1757421251.7929554,
            "update_time": 1757421254.5116408,
            "code": "from matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\n\r\n# Assign IDs per glyph\r\ndef enumerate_points(pts, prefix):\r\n    # Sort points for consistent numbering: left-to-right, bottom-to-top\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(pts, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    labeled = [(i+1, p[0], p[1]) for i, p in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i:02d}\", x, y) for (i, x, y) in labeled]\r\n\r\nLZ = enumerate_points(pts_Z, \"走\")\r\nL1 = enumerate_points(pts_O, \"1\")\r\nL5 = enumerate_points(pts_F, \"5\")\r\nL0 = enumerate_points(pts_0, \"0\")\r\n\r\ntotal = len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)\r\n\r\npdf_path = \"/mnt/data/走150_人文字レイアウト_20x10m_配置図.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path) as pdf:\r\n    # Page 1: Overview map\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))  # A4 landscape\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, 21):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, 10], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, 11):\r\n        ax.plot([0, 20], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,20,20,0,0],[0,0,10,10,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Draw glyph stroke outlines (rectangles) for reference\r\n    for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in Z + O + F + Z0_outer:\r\n        rect = plt.Rectangle((rx, ry), rw, rh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0)\r\n        ax.add_patch(rect)\r\n    # Inner hole of 0\r\n    (irx, iry, irw, irh) = zero_inner\r\n    rect_in = plt.Rectangle((irx, iry), irw, irh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\")\r\n    ax.add_patch(rect_in)\r\n    # Plot points with small markers and labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=2)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.05, y+0.05, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    plot_group(LZ); plot_group(L1); plot_group(L5); plot_group(L0)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, 20.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, 10.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"走150 人文字レイアウト｜20m×10m｜1マス=1m｜配置密度=0.4mピッチ\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"メートル（横）\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"メートル（縦）\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: Counts and instructions\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    lines = []\r\n    lines.append(\"【人数内訳】（合計: {}名）\".format(total))\r\n    lines.append(\"・走: {}名\".format(len(LZ)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・1 : {}名\".format(len(L1)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・5 : {}名\".format(len(L5)))\r\n    lines.append(\"・0 : {}名\".format(len(L0)))\r\n    lines.append(\"\")\r\n    lines.append(\"【当日運用の手順（要約）】\")\r\n    lines.append(\"1) 校庭に外枠20m×10mをロープで作成、1m間隔でマーキング。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"2) PDFの矩形線に沿ってチョークで輪郭を描画（「0」の内側矩形は立入禁止）。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"3) 児童=『走』『1』『5』内側中心、 大人=『走』『0』外周強調に配置。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"4) 呼び出し: グループごとに「走-01〜」「1-01〜」の順で整列。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"5) ドローン高度20〜30m、真下撮影。上空確認→微調整→本番。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"\")\r\n    lines.append(\"【注意】\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・配置点は0.4mピッチ。混雑時は交互に間引き可（偶数番号を省く等）。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・安全最優先。走らない。ロープ・ペグに注意。\")\r\n    lines.append(\"・強風時はドローン運用を中止し、梯子/屋上からの撮影に切替。\")\r\n    txt = \"\\n\".join(lines)\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path\n",
            "end_time": 1757421254.5116408,

      "parent": "c13e5659-6c93-44b9-944e-2279f6b7c6d2",

    "72ca1e39-ea13-4783-90ef-854a3262c831": {
      "id": "72ca1e39-ea13-4783-90ef-854a3262c831",
      "message": {
        "id": "72ca1e39-ea13-4783-90ef-854a3262c831",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421254.519624,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# Regenerate with ASCII-safe labels and text to avoid missing font glyphs\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\n\r\ndef enumerate_points_ascii(pts, prefix):\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(pts, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i+1:02d}\", p[0], p[1]) for i, p in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n\r\nLZ = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_Z, \"Z\")  # Z = 走\r\nL1 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_O, \"1\")\r\nL5 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_F, \"5\")\r\nL0 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_0, \"0\")\r\n\r\npdf_path_ascii = \"/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x10m_GRID.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path_ascii) as pdf:\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, 21):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, 10], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, 11):\r\n        ax.plot([0, 20], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,20,20,0,0],[0,0,10,10,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Outlines\r\n    for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in Z + O + F + Z0_outer:\r\n        rect = plt.Rectangle((rx, ry), rw, rh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0)\r\n        ax.add_patch(rect)\r\n    (irx, iry, irw, irh) = zero_inner\r\n    rect_in = plt.Rectangle((irx, iry), irw, irh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\")\r\n    ax.add_patch(rect_in)\r\n    # Points + labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=2)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.05, y+0.05, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    for group in (LZ, L1, L5, L0):\r\n        plot_group(group)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, 20.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, 10.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"SOU 150 (Z=走) | 20m x 10m | Grid 1m, Positions 0.4m pitch\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"meters (X)\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"meters (Y)\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: legend and counts\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    txt = f\"\"\"LEGEND\r\n- Z = Kanji '走' (SOU)\r\n- 1,5,0 = numerals\r\n- Spacing: 0.4 m pitch within 1.0 m stroke bands\r\n- Field: 20 m (W) x 10 m (H), 1 m grid\r\n\r\nHEADCOUNT\r\n- Z (走): {len(LZ)} persons\r\n- 1    : {len(L1)} persons\r\n- 5    : {len(L5)} persons\r\n- 0    : {len(L0)} persons\r\nTOTAL : {len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)} persons\r\n\r\nRUN OF SHOW (quick)\r\n1) Rope a 20x10 m rectangle; mark every 1 m.\r\n2) Chalk along rectangle outlines; inner dashed box of '0' is keep-out.\r\n3) Children -> inner cores; Adults -> outer edges (Z and 0) for bold outline.\r\n4) Call by tags: Z-01.., 1-01.., 5-01.., 0-01..\r\n5) Drone at 20–30 m, nadir shot; adjust; take.\r\n\r\nSAFETY\r\n- No running. Watch ropes/pegs.\r\n- Strong wind: avoid drone; use ladder/roof vantage.\"\"\"\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path_ascii\n"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": false,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "citations": [],

ax.set_ylim(-0.5, 10.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"SOU 150 (Z=走) | 20m x 10m | Grid 1m, Positions 0.4m pitch\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"meters (X)\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"meters (Y)\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: legend and counts\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    txt = f\"\"\"LEGEND\r\n- Z = Kanji '走' (SOU)\r\n- 1,5,0 = numerals\r\n- Spacing: 0.4 m pitch within 1.0 m stroke bands\r\n- Field: 20 m (W) x 10 m (H), 1 m grid\r\n\r\nHEADCOUNT\r\n- Z (走): {len(LZ)} persons\r\n- 1    : {len(L1)} persons\r\n- 5    : {len(L5)} persons\r\n- 0    : {len(L0)} persons\r\nTOTAL : {len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)} persons\r\n\r\nRUN OF SHOW (quick)\r\n1) Rope a 20x10 m rectangle;     "a3a0c956-8119-4f8c-a781-aa6760756b68": {
      "id": "a3a0c956-8119-4f8c-a781-aa6760756b68",
      "message": {
        "id": "a3a0c956-8119-4f8c-a781-aa6760756b68",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421279.885663,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "やりました。**「走150」人文字の本番用レイアウト**を実寸で組みました（フィールド：**横20m × 縦10m**／1m方眼、**0.4mピッチ**で立ち位置割り）。PDFはすぐ配布・印刷可能です。\n\n- 配置図（日本語ラベル版・一部環境で文字化けの可能性）\n  - [ダウンロード（PDF）](sandbox:/mnt/data/走150_人文字レイアウト_20x10m_配置図.pdf)\n- 配置図（ASCIIラベル版：**Z=走** 表記／どの環境でも表示安定）\n  - [ダウンロード（PDF）](sandbox:/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x10m_GRID.pdf)\n\n### 設計サマリー\n- 文字割：**「走」+「1」「5」「0」**（横並び）\n- ストローク幅：概ね **1.0m**（人列が線に見える太さ）\n- 立ち位置密度：**0.4mピッチ**（混雑時は番号の偶数だけ立たせる等で間引き可）\n- 目安人数（実測）\n  - 走：**64名**\n  - 1：**23名**\n  - 5：**39名**\n  - 0：**22名**\n  - **合計：148名**（余剰の**2名**は「走」の外周角で太さ補強に回してください）\n\n### 当日の運用（現場オペレーション）\n1) **基準枠を作る**  \n　外周**20m×10m**をロープで四角取り→**1mごと**に地面へマーキング。\n\n2) **輪郭を描く**  \n　PDFの矩形線に沿ってチョークで太線を引く。  \n　「0」は内側の破線矩形が**立入禁止（抜き）**です。\n\n3) **並べ方（役割分担）**  \n- 児童：**内側中心〜中間**を埋める（密になりすぎる場合は**番号の奇数のみ**にする等で間引き）  \n- 大人：**外周の輪郭**を担当（線が太くクッキリ見える）  \n- 呼び出しは**文字ごと**に「走（Z）→1→5→0」の順で、タグ番号順に整列。  \n  - ASCII版PDFのタグ表記：**Z-01, 1-01, 5-01, 0-01…**\n\n4) **撮影**  \n　ドローン**高度20〜30m**、真下（ナディア）でフレーミング→上空確認→微調整→本番。  \n　強風時は**ドローン中止**、脚立／屋上からの真上に近いアングルで代替。\n\n### 安全と品質の注意\n- **安全最優先**：走らない。ロープ・ペグ周りに見張り配置。  \n- **間引き運用**：密と感じたら**番号の偶数（or奇数）のみ**配置で即座に密度調整。  \n- **見栄え**：背の高い人（大人・高学年）は**外周**、低学年は**内側**へ。  \n- **歪み対策**：横長に写りがちなので、現場でドローン画面を見ながら**縦比を微修正**。\n\n---\n\n必要なら、**学年別・クラス別の割当表（CSV）**も即作ります（例：1年=Z-01〜Z-16、2年=Z-17〜…）。次はクラス配分表の作成に進めますか？"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": true,
        "weight": 1,

    "b481c654-69a2-4377-b7bf-764c7d352382": {
      "id": "b481c654-69a2-4377-b7bf-764c7d352382",
      "message": {
        "id": "b481c654-69a2-4377-b7bf-764c7d352382",
        "author": {

    "9b942f65-9925-4892-8ccc-81f0c6a662c6": {
      "id": "9b942f65-9925-4892-8ccc-81f0c6a662c6",
      "message": {
        "id": "9b942f65-9925-4892-8ccc-81f0c6a662c6",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421677.781844,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "了解です。  \nGoogle マップから正確な **校庭や敷地の実寸サイズ**（例：横20m × 縦10m）を測るには、  \n**Google マップの「距離測定」機能**を使うのが最も簡単で精度も高いです。  \nPC・スマホどちらでも可能ですが、PCの方が細かい座標を指定しやすいです。  \nここでは **小学校校庭の人文字用レイアウト**を前提に、実寸を取る手順をまとめます。\n\n---\n\n## **1. Google マップで距離を測る（PC版）**\n### 手順\n1. **Googleマップを開く**  \n   [https://maps.google.com/](https://maps.google.com/)\n2. **校庭の場所を検索**  \n   例：「走潟小学校」など。\n3. **計測開始**  \n   - 校庭の端を右クリック  \n   - **「距離を測定」**を選択。\n4. **計測ポイントを打つ**  \n   - 角→角へクリックして、線を引きます。\n   - クリックすると即座に距離が表示。\n5. **長方形を測る場合**  \n   - 4点を順にクリックして閉じると、  \n     「周囲の長さ＋面積」まで表示されます。\n\n### 精度の目安\n- Googleマップ航空写真の解像度は **±50cm程度**  \n- 校庭レベルでは **誤差は1〜2%程度**なので実用レベルです。\n\n---\n\n## **2. Google マップで距離を測る（スマホ版）**\n### 手順（iPhone/Android共通）\n1. **Googleマップアプリを起動**\n2. **校庭の場所を表示**\n3. 計測開始：\n   - 校庭の端を**長押し** → ピンを立てる\n   - 画面下のピン名をタップ → **「距離を測定」**を選択。\n4. **もう一方の角までスワイプして測定**\n   - 線が表示され、距離が下に出る。\n5. 複数点をクリックすると、校庭全体の面積も測定可能。\n\n---\n\n## **3. 校庭を正確に使うためのコツ**\n### ① ドローン画像と組み合わせるとさらに精度UP\n- 実測後、ドローンで上空から真下に写真撮影\n- 校庭の基準線を写して、正しい比率でレイアウト図に反映可能。\n\n### ② ストリートビューは使わない\n- 地上写真からの距離推定は誤差が大きいので避ける。\n\n### ③ Google Earth Pro（PCアプリ）を使うと最強\n- 無料でダウンロード可能\n- **正確な測量ツール**が付属\n- 複数ポイントを設定して**正確な多角形面積**を出せる\n- ドローン撮影予定がある場合はGoogle Earthでレイアウト下書きをすると便利。\n\n---\n\n## **4. 実践アドバイス（今回のケース）**\n「走150」人文字の場合、  \n校庭20m×10mの実寸が確かならこのままレイアウト図でOKですが、  \nもし±1m以上ズレていると文字バランスが崩れます。\n\n**おすすめの流れ**\n1. Googleマップで校庭の実測 → 4隅の距離を取る  \n2. 数値をレイアウト図に反映  \n3. ドローンで試し撮影 → ズレがあれば調整\n\n---\n\n## **次にできること**\nもし希望があれば、  \n- Googleマップで計測した座標を入力すると、**自動で正確なレイアウト図を再生成**できます。  \n- さらに、**Google Earth用のKMLファイル**を作れば、上空写真に人文字を重ねてシミュレーション可能です。\n\nやりますか？  \n「走150」の人文字をGoogle Earth上にシミュレーションして、空撮イメージで事前チェックしますか？"
          ]
        },

    "8a5234ad-621a-4000-8c6d-f3a79205bac5": {
      "id": "8a5234ad-621a-4000-8c6d-f3a79205bac5",
      "message": {
        "id": "8a5234ad-621a-4000-8c6d-f3a79205bac5",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421827.430642,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# Rebuild the layout for a 20m (W) x 17m (H) field.\r\n# Goal: keep total points close to 150 persons.\r\n# We'll start with 0.5m pitch and adjust.\r\n\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0  # field size in meters\r\n\r\ndef rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh):\r\n    return (x >= rx) and (x <= rx+rw) and (y >= ry) and (y <= ry+rh)\r\n\r\ndef make_points_in_rects(rects, spacing=1.0):\r\n    if not rects:\r\n        return []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in rects)\r\n    pts = []\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            if any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in rects):\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\ndef points_for_ring(outer_rects, inner_rect, spacing=1.0):\r\n    pts = []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            in_outer = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in outer_rects)\r\n            in_inner = rect_contains(x, y, *inner_rect)\r\n            if in_outer and not in_inner:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters (reuse widths, but center vertically within H=17)\r\ngap = 0.5\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 4.0, 4.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.7, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\nt = 1.0\r\n\r\nZ = []\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.2, zou_y + zou_h - t - 0.2, zou_w - 0.4, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.3, zou_y + 2.1, zou_w - 0.6, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + (zou_w - t)/2, zou_y + 0.6, t, 2.8))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.5, zou_y + 0.4, zou_w - 1.0, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.6, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + zou_w - 0.6 - t, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n\r\nnum_w, num_h = 2.5, 3.0\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\n\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\nO = []\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - t/2, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nO.append((one_x + 0.3, base_y, num_w - 0.6, t))\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - 0.7, base_y + num_h - t, 1.4, t))\r\n\r\nF = []\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2 - t/2, num_w*0.8, t))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w*0.2, base_y, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n\r\nZ0_outer = []\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, num_w, t))\r\n\r\nzero_inner_margin = 0.6\r\nzero_inner = (zero_x + zero_inner_margin, base_y + zero_inner_margin,\r\n              num_w - 2*zero_inner_margin, num_h - 2*zero_inner_margin)\r\n\r\ndef counts_for_spacing(sp):\r\n    pts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=sp)\r\n    return len(pts_Z), len(pts_O), len(pts_F), len(pts_0), len(pts_Z)+len(pts_O)+len(pts_F)+len(pts_0)\r\n\r\nfor sp in [0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6]:\r\n    print(sp, counts_for_spacing(sp))\n"
          ]
        },

            "update_time": 1757421888.0032277,
            "code": "# Rebuild the layout for a 20m (W) x 17m (H) field.\r\n# Goal: keep total points close to 150 persons.\r\n# We'll start with 0.5m pitch and adjust.\r\n\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0  # field size in meters\r\n\r\ndef rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh):\r\n    return (x >= rx) and (x <= rx+rw) and (y >= ry) and (y <= ry+rh)\r\n\r\ndef make_points_in_rects(rects, spacing=1.0):\r\n    if not rects:\r\n        return []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in rects)\r\n    pts = []\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            if any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in rects):\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\ndef points_for_ring(outer_rects, inner_rect, spacing=1.0):\r\n    pts = []\r\n    minx = min(r[0] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    miny = min(r[1] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxx = max(r[0]+r[2] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    maxy = max(r[1]+r[3] for r in outer_rects)\r\n    x = minx + spacing/2.0\r\n    while x <= maxx - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n        y = miny + spacing/2.0\r\n        while y <= maxy - spacing/2.0 + 1e-9:\r\n            in_outer = any(rect_contains(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in outer_rects)\r\n            in_inner = rect_contains(x, y, *inner_rect)\r\n            if in_outer and not in_inner:\r\n                pts.append((x, y))\r\n            y += spacing\r\n        x += spacing\r\n    return pts\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters (reuse widths, but center vertically within H=17)\r\ngap = 0.5\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 4.0, 4.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.7, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\nt = 1.0\r\n\r\nZ = []\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.2, zou_y + zou_h - t - 0.2, zou_w - 0.4, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.3, zou_y + 2.1, zou_w - 0.6, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + (zou_w - t)/2, zou_y + 0.6, t, 2.8))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.5, zou_y + 0.4, zou_w - 1.0, t))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + 0.6, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\nZ.append((zou_x + zou_w - 0.6 - t, zou_y + 1.1, t, 1.2))\r\n\r\nnum_w, num_h = 2.5, 3.0\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\n\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\nO = []\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - t/2, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nO.append((one_x + 0.3, base_y, num_w - 0.6, t))\r\nO.append((one_x + num_w/2 - 0.7, base_y + num_h - t, 1.4, t))\r\n\r\nF = []\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x, base_y + num_h/2 - t/2, num_w*0.8, t))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h/2))\r\nF.append((five_x + num_w*0.2, base_y, num_w*0.8, t))\r\n\r\nZ0_outer = []\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x + num_w - t, base_y, t, num_h))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y + num_h - t, num_w, t))\r\nZ0_outer.append((zero_x, base_y, num_w, t))\r\n\r\nzero_inner_margin = 0.6\r\nzero_inner = (zero_x + zero_inner_margin, base_y + zero_inner_margin,\r\n              num_w - 2*zero_inner_margin, num_h - 2*zero_inner_margin)\r\n\r\ndef counts_for_spacing(sp):\r\n    pts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=sp)\r\n    pts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=sp)\r\n    return len(pts_Z), len(pts_O), len(pts_F), len(pts_0), len(pts_Z)+len(pts_O)+len(pts_F)+len(pts_0)\r\n\r\nfor sp in [0.4, 0.45, 0.5, 0.55, 0.6]:\r\n    print(sp, counts_for_spacing(sp))\n",
            "end_time": 1757421888.0032277,
            "final_expression_output": null,
            "in_kernel_exception": null,
            "system_exception": null,

              }
            ],
            "jupyter_messages": [

    "3a058102-6e58-4cb9-9db0-dc800544d9cb": {
      "id": "3a058102-6e58-4cb9-9db0-dc800544d9cb",
      "message": {
        "id": "3a058102-6e58-4cb9-9db0-dc800544d9cb",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421888.013063,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# Great—0.4m pitch yields ~149, which matches 150 people goal.\r\n# Generate the updated 20x17m PDF (ASCII-stable labels).\r\n\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\n\r\ndef enumerate_points_ascii(pts, prefix):\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(pts, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i+1:02d}\", p[0], p[1]) for i, p in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n\r\n# Recompute for 0.4m pitch\r\nsp = 0.4\r\npts_Z = make_points_in_rects(Z, spacing=sp)\r\npts_O = make_points_in_rects(O, spacing=sp)\r\npts_F = make_points_in_rects(F, spacing=sp)\r\npts_0 = points_for_ring(Z0_outer, zero_inner, spacing=sp)\r\n\r\nLZ = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_Z, \"Z\")  # Z = 走\r\nL1 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_O, \"1\")\r\nL5 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_F, \"5\")\r\nL0 = enumerate_points_ascii(pts_0, \"0\")\r\n\r\npdf_path_ascii = \"/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_GRID.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path_ascii) as pdf:\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, int(W)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, H], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, int(H)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([0, W], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,W,W,0,0],[0,0,H,H,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Outlines\r\n    for (rx, ry, rw, rh) in Z + O + F + Z0_outer:\r\n        rect = plt.Rectangle((rx, ry), rw, rh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0)\r\n        ax.add_patch(rect)\r\n    (irx, iry, irw, irh) = zero_inner\r\n    rect_in = plt.Rectangle((irx, iry), irw, irh, fill=False, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\")\r\n    ax.add_patch(rect_in)\r\n    # Points + labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=2)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.05, y+0.05, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    for group in (LZ, L1, L5, L0):\r\n        plot_group(group)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, W+0.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, H+0.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"SOU 150 (Z=走) | 20m x 17m | Grid 1m, Positions 0.4m pitch\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"meters (X)\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"meters (Y)\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: legend and counts\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    txt = f\"\"\"LEGEND\r\n- Z = Kanji '走' (SOU)\r\n- 1,5,0 = numerals\r\n- Spacing: 0.4 m pitch within 1.0 m stroke bands\r\n- Field: 20 m (W) x 17 m (H), 1 m grid\r\n\r\nHEADCOUNT\r\n- Z (走): {len(LZ)} persons\r\n- 1    : {len(L1)} persons\r\n- 5    : {len(L5)} persons\r\n- 0    : {len(L0)} persons\r\nTOTAL : {len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)} persons\r\n\r\nRUN OF SHOW (quick)\r\n1) Rope a 20x17 m rectangle; mark every 1 m.\r\n2) Chalk along rectangle outlines; inner dashed box of '0' is keep-out.\r\n3) Children -> inner cores; Adults -> outer edges (Z and 0) for bold outline.\r\n4) Call by tags: Z-01.., 1-01.., 5-01.., 0-01..\r\n5) Drone at ~25–35 m, nadir shot; adjust; take.\r\n\r\nSAFETY\r\n- No running. Watch ropes/pegs.\r\n- Strong wind: avoid drone; use ladder/roof vantage.\"\"\"\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path_ascii, len(LZ), len(L1), len(L5), len(L0), len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)\n"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",

    "47b3a60e-9585-4483-99c2-973dc99751c5": {
      "id": "47b3a60e-9585-4483-99c2-973dc99751c5",
      "message": {
        "id": "47b3a60e-9585-4483-99c2-973dc99751c5",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421900.22234,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "了解。**計測枠：縦17m × 横20m** に合わせて、レイアウトを即座にアップデートしました。  \n人の立ち位置は **0.4mピッチ**に再設計し、**ほぼジャスト150名（計149名）**に収めています。\n\n- 新レイアウト（20m×17m／1m方眼・ASCII安定版）  \n  [ダウンロード（PDF）](sandbox:/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_GRID.pdf)\n\n### 人数内訳（0.4mピッチ）\n- 走（Z表記）：**64名**  \n- 1：**24名**  \n- 5：**39名**  \n- 0：**22名**  \n- **合計：149名**（余り1名は「走」外周の角に増強配置すると線が太く見えます）\n\n### 現場オペレーション（17m版）\n1) **外枠ロープ**：20×17mをロープで取り、**1m間隔**にマーキング。  \n2) **チョークで輪郭**：PDFの矩形どおり。**0の内側破線は立入禁止**（抜き）  \n3) **配置**：児童=内側中心〜中間／大人=外周強調（「走」「0」の輪郭でコントラストUP）  \n4) **呼び出し**：**Z-01… → 1-01… → 5-01… → 0-01…** の順に番号で整列  \n5) **撮影**：高度**25〜35m**で真下撮影→上空確認→微調整→本番\n\n### その先の最適化\n- **密度調整**：混雑や安全配慮が必要なら、**偶数番号のみ**立たせて即時に間引き可能。  \n- **クラス割当CSV**を作れば、配布→点呼が一気に楽になります（例：1年=Z-01〜Z-16…）。  \n\nクラス別の**割当表（CSV）**、作成しますか？ 児童100名＋大人50名の内訳（学年・担当）をもらえれば、PDFと整合した番号で即出力します。"
          ]
        },

    "7b521a41-8b7b-437f-bced-68c9384deecd": {
      "id": "7b521a41-8b7b-437f-bced-68c9384deecd",
      "message": {
        "id": "7b521a41-8b7b-437f-bced-68c9384deecd",

      "parent": "11d1a482-a5e4-4ccb-be0b-98ac3996f61c",

        "d3cf8b0e-a8d0-414b-ac11-64ecbf4f2db9"

    },
    "d3cf8b0e-a8d0-414b-ac11-64ecbf4f2db9": {
      "id": "d3cf8b0e-a8d0-414b-ac11-64ecbf4f2db9",
      "message": {
        "id": "d3cf8b0e-a8d0-414b-ac11-64ecbf4f2db9",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757421998.784382,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# Rebuild layout using an \"outline/rail\" method for clearer human placement.\r\n# - Field: 20m (W) x 17m (H)\r\n# - Use centerline rails for each stroke, with 0.6m person pitch (center-to-center).\r\n# - For bold strokes, use two parallel rails separated by 0.6m.\r\n#\r\n# We'll output a new PDF with numbered tags and a summary of counts.\r\n\r\nimport math\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0\r\n\r\nPITCH = 0.6          # center-to-center spacing (approx human width+buffer)\r\nRAIL_GAP = 0.6       # distance between parallel rails for bold strokes\r\nDOT_SIZE = 3         # marker size\r\n\r\ndef unit(vx, vy):\r\n    n = math.hypot(vx, vy)\r\n    if n == 0:\r\n        return 0.0, 0.0\r\n    return vx/n, vy/n\r\n\r\ndef add_line(points, x1, y1, x2, y2, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    # Create points along a line segment with optional parallel rails (offset normal)\r\n    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1\r\n    ux, uy = unit(dx, dy)\r\n    # normal vector\r\n    nx, ny = -uy, ux\r\n    length = math.hypot(dx, dy)\r\n    if length == 0: \r\n        return\r\n    npts = max(1, int(length / pitch) + 1)\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        # offset: center rails around the centerline (e.g., for 2 rails: -gap/2 and +gap/2)\r\n        offset = ( (r - (rails-1)/2.0) * rail_gap )\r\n        for i in range(npts):\r\n            t = min(1.0, i * pitch / length)\r\n            x = x1 + t*dx + offset*nx\r\n            y = y1 + t*dy + offset*ny\r\n            if 0 <= x <= W and 0 <= y <= H:\r\n                points.append((x, y))\r\n\r\ndef add_rect_outline(points, x, y, w, h, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP, closed=True):\r\n    # Outline rectangle with rails (parallel offsets: outer and/or inner). We'll only do rails on center outline by offset normal outward/inward isn't trivial for corners, so we draw multiple concentric rectangles by insetting.\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        inset = (r - (rails-1)/2.0)* (rail_gap)\r\n        # draw inset rectangle (positive inset shrinks)\r\n        rx, ry = x + inset, y + inset\r\n        rw, rh = w - 2*inset, h - 2*inset\r\n        if rw <= 0 or rh <= 0: \r\n            continue\r\n        # top\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # right\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # bottom\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry, rx, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # left\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry, rx, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n\r\ndef dedup(points, tol=0.15):\r\n    # Deduplicate points that are too close to each other (due to corners/overlaps)\r\n    out = []\r\n    for x, y in points:\r\n        if all((x-x2)**2 + (y-y2)**2 > tol**2 for x2, y2 in out):\r\n            out.append((x, y))\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n# Layout: place \"走\" at left, numerals to the right.\r\ngap = 0.7\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 6.0, 6.0       # bigger kanji for readability in 17m height\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.8, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\n\r\nnum_w, num_h = 3.2, 4.5       # taller numerals for 17m\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\n# Build points per glyph\r\nPZ, P1, P5, P0 = [], [], [], []\r\n\r\n# --- \"走\" strokes as polylines (simplified, bold where needed) ---\r\n# Top bar\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, zou_x+zou_w-0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, rails=2)\r\n# Middle bar\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, zou_x+zou_w-0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Center vertical\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+0.9, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+zou_h-1.0, rails=2)\r\n# Bottom base\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.8, zou_y+0.8, zou_x+zou_w-0.8, zou_y+0.8, rails=2)\r\n# Short verticals (legs)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\n\r\nPZ = dedup(PZ)\r\n\r\n# --- \"1\" as a vertical + base + small top serif ---\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + 0.3, base_y, one_x + num_w-0.3, base_y, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0 - 0.7, base_y+num_h, one_x + num_w/2.0 + 0.7, base_y+num_h, rails=1)\r\nP1 = dedup(P1)\r\n\r\n# --- \"5\" outline-ish ---\r\n# Top bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\n# Left upper vertical\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Middle bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, five_x+num_w*0.8, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Right lower vertical\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Bottom bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w*0.2, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, rails=2)\r\nP5 = dedup(P5)\r\n\r\n# --- \"0\" as ring (outer + inner) ---\r\n# Outer rectangle outline (rounded feel via rails)\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x, base_y, num_w, num_h, rails=2)\r\n# Inner rectangle outline (hole)\r\ninner_margin = 0.7\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x+inner_margin, base_y+inner_margin, num_w-2*inner_margin, num_h-2*inner_margin, rails=1)\r\nP0 = dedup(P0)\r\n\r\n# Summaries\r\ncounts = (len(PZ), len(P1), len(P5), len(P0), len(PZ)+len(P1)+len(P5)+len(P0))\r\n\r\n# Build PDF\r\ndef label_points(points, prefix):\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i+1:02d}\", x, y) for i,(x,y) in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n\r\nLZ = label_points(PZ, \"Z\")\r\nL1 = label_points(P1, \"1\")\r\nL5 = label_points(P5, \"5\")\r\nL0 = label_points(P0, \"0\")\r\n\r\npdf_path = \"/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_OUTLINE_0p6pitch.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path) as pdf:\r\n    # Page 1: Map\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, int(W)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, H], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, int(H)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([0, W], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,W,W,0,0],[0,0,H,H,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Draw rough bounding boxes for glyphs\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((zou_x, zou_y), zou_w, zou_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((one_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((five_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((zero_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    # Plot points and labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=DOT_SIZE)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.07, y+0.07, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    for group in (LZ, L1, L5, L0):\r\n        plot_group(group)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, W+0.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, H+0.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"SOU 150 (Z=走) | Outline rails | 20m x 17m | pitch=0.6m\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"meters (X)\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"meters (Y)\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: counts + guidance\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    txt = f\"\"\"PLACEMENT METHOD: OUTLINE RAILS\r\n- People stand along rails (centerlines), spacing ≈ {PITCH} m.\r\n- Bold strokes use parallel rails at {RAIL_GAP} m gap for thickness.\r\n\r\nFIELD\r\n- {W} m (W) x {H} m (H), 1 m ground grid.\r\n\r\nHEADCOUNT\r\n- Z (走): {len(LZ)} persons\r\n- 1    : {len(L1)} persons\r\n- 5    : {len(L5)} persons\r\n- 0    : {len(L0)} persons\r\nTOTAL : {len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)} persons\r\n\r\nOPERATIONS\r\n- Children: inner rails; Adults: outer rails (Z & 0 perimeter) for strong outline.\r\n- Call by tags: Z-01.., 1-01.., 5-01.., 0-01..\r\n- Adjust live by skipping every Nth tag if density too high.\r\n\r\nSAFETY\r\n- Keep clear space at corners; no running; monitor ropes/pegs.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path, counts\n"
          ]
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": false,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "citations": [],

          "text": "/tmp/ipykernel_11/1917534387.py:166: UserWarning: Glyph 36208 (\\N{CJK UNIFIED IDEOGRAPH-8D70}) missing from current font.\n  pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\n/tmp/ipykernel_11/1917534387.py:195: UserWarning: Glyph 36208 (\\N{CJK UNIFIED IDEOGRAPH-8D70}) missing from current font.\n  pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\n('/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_OUTLINE_0p6pitch.pdf', (71, 24, 48, 72, 215))"

          "is_complete": true,
          "aggregate_result": {
            "status": "success",
            "run_id": "3cefab03-9217-40bf-bbc2-94202c6fd3c9",
            "start_time": 1757422111.745961,
            "update_time": 1757422115.5988848,
            "code": "# Rebuild layout using an \"outline/rail\" method for clearer human placement.\r\n# - Field: 20m (W) x 17m (H)\r\n# - Use centerline rails for each stroke, with 0.6m person pitch (center-to-center).\r\n# - For bold strokes, use two parallel rails separated by 0.6m.\r\n#\r\n# We'll output a new PDF with numbered tags and a summary of counts.\r\n\r\nimport math\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0\r\n\r\nPITCH = 0.6          # center-to-center spacing (approx human width+buffer)\r\nRAIL_GAP = 0.6       # distance between parallel rails for bold strokes\r\nDOT_SIZE = 3         # marker size\r\n\r\ndef unit(vx, vy):\r\n    n = math.hypot(vx, vy)\r\n    if n == 0:\r\n        return 0.0, 0.0\r\n    return vx/n, vy/n\r\n\r\ndef add_line(points, x1, y1, x2, y2, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    # Create points along a line segment with optional parallel rails (offset normal)\r\n    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1\r\n    ux, uy = unit(dx, dy)\r\n    # normal vector\r\n    nx, ny = -uy, ux\r\n    length = math.hypot(dx, dy)\r\n    if length == 0: \r\n        return\r\n    npts = max(1, int(length / pitch) + 1)\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        # offset: center rails around the centerline (e.g., for 2 rails: -gap/2 and +gap/2)\r\n        offset = ( (r - (rails-1)/2.0) * rail_gap )\r\n        for i in range(npts):\r\n            t = min(1.0, i * pitch / length)\r\n            x = x1 + t*dx + offset*nx\r\n            y = y1 + t*dy + offset*ny\r\n            if 0 <= x <= W and 0 <= y <= H:\r\n                points.append((x, y))\r\n\r\ndef add_rect_outline(points, x, y, w, h, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP, closed=True):\r\n    # Outline rectangle with rails (parallel offsets: outer and/or inner). We'll only do rails on center outline by offset normal outward/inward isn't trivial for corners, so we draw multiple concentric rectangles by insetting.\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        inset = (r - (rails-1)/2.0)* (rail_gap)\r\n        # draw inset rectangle (positive inset shrinks)\r\n        rx, ry = x + inset, y + inset\r\n        rw, rh = w - 2*inset, h - 2*inset\r\n        if rw <= 0 or rh <= 0: \r\n            continue\r\n        # top\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # right\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # bottom\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry, rx, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        # left\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry, rx, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n\r\ndef dedup(points, tol=0.15):\r\n    # Deduplicate points that are too close to each other (due to corners/overlaps)\r\n    out = []\r\n    for x, y in points:\r\n        if all((x-x2)**2 + (y-y2)**2 > tol**2 for x2, y2 in out):\r\n            out.append((x, y))\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n# Layout: place \"走\" at left, numerals to the right.\r\ngap = 0.7\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 6.0, 6.0       # bigger kanji for readability in 17m height\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.8, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\n\r\nnum_w, num_h = 3.2, 4.5       # taller numerals for 17m\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\n# Build points per glyph\r\nPZ, P1, P5, P0 = [], [], [], []\r\n\r\n# --- \"走\" strokes as polylines (simplified, bold where needed) ---\r\n# Top bar\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, zou_x+zou_w-0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, rails=2)\r\n# Middle bar\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, zou_x+zou_w-0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Center vertical\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+0.9, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+zou_h-1.0, rails=2)\r\n# Bottom base\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.8, zou_y+0.8, zou_x+zou_w-0.8, zou_y+0.8, rails=2)\r\n# Short verticals (legs)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\n\r\nPZ = dedup(PZ)\r\n\r\n# --- \"1\" as a vertical + base + small top serif ---\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + 0.3, base_y, one_x + num_w-0.3, base_y, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0 - 0.7, base_y+num_h, one_x + num_w/2.0 + 0.7, base_y+num_h, rails=1)\r\nP1 = dedup(P1)\r\n\r\n# --- \"5\" outline-ish ---\r\n# Top bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\n# Left upper vertical\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Middle bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, five_x+num_w*0.8, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Right lower vertical\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\n# Bottom bar\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w*0.2, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, rails=2)\r\nP5 = dedup(P5)\r\n\r\n# --- \"0\" as ring (outer + inner) ---\r\n# Outer rectangle outline (rounded feel via rails)\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x, base_y, num_w, num_h, rails=2)\r\n# Inner rectangle outline (hole)\r\ninner_margin = 0.7\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x+inner_margin, base_y+inner_margin, num_w-2*inner_margin, num_h-2*inner_margin, rails=1)\r\nP0 = dedup(P0)\r\n\r\n# Summaries\r\ncounts = (len(PZ), len(P1), len(P5), len(P0), len(PZ)+len(P1)+len(P5)+len(P0))\r\n\r\n# Build PDF\r\ndef label_points(points, prefix):\r\n    pts_sorted = sorted(points, key=lambda p: (p[0], p[1]))\r\n    return [(f\"{prefix}-{i+1:02d}\", x, y) for i,(x,y) in enumerate(pts_sorted)]\r\n\r\nLZ = label_points(PZ, \"Z\")\r\nL1 = label_points(P1, \"1\")\r\nL5 = label_points(P5, \"5\")\r\nL0 = label_points(P0, \"0\")\r\n\r\npdf_path = \"/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_OUTLINE_0p6pitch.pdf\"\r\nwith PdfPages(pdf_path) as pdf:\r\n    # Page 1: Map\r\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    # Grid\r\n    for x in range(0, int(W)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([x, x], [0, H], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    for y in range(0, int(H)+1):\r\n        ax.plot([0, W], [y, y], linewidth=0.5)\r\n    # Boundary\r\n    ax.plot([0,W,W,0,0],[0,0,H,H,0], linewidth=1.5)\r\n    # Draw rough bounding boxes for glyphs\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((zou_x, zou_y), zou_w, zou_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((one_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((five_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    ax.add_patch(plt.Rectangle((zero_x, base_y), num_w, num_h, fill=False, linewidth=1.0))\r\n    # Plot points and labels\r\n    def plot_group(labeled_pts):\r\n        for tag, x, y in labeled_pts:\r\n            ax.plot(x, y, marker='o', markersize=DOT_SIZE)\r\n            ax.text(x+0.07, y+0.07, tag, fontsize=4)\r\n    for group in (LZ, L1, L5, L0):\r\n        plot_group(group)\r\n    ax.set_aspect('equal', adjustable='box')\r\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, W+0.5); ax.set_ylim(-0.5, H+0.5)\r\n    ax.set_title(\"SOU 150 (Z=走) | Outline rails | 20m x 17m | pitch=0.6m\")\r\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"meters (X)\"); ax.set_ylabel(\"meters (Y)\")\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig)\r\n\r\n    # Page 2: counts + guidance\r\n    fig2, ax2 = plt.subplots(figsize=(11.69, 8.27))\r\n    ax2.axis('off')\r\n    txt = f\"\"\"PLACEMENT METHOD: OUTLINE RAILS\r\n- People stand along rails (centerlines), spacing ≈ {PITCH} m.\r\n- Bold strokes use parallel rails at {RAIL_GAP} m gap for thickness.\r\n\r\nFIELD\r\n- {W} m (W) x {H} m (H), 1 m ground grid.\r\n\r\nHEADCOUNT\r\n- Z (走): {len(LZ)} persons\r\n- 1    : {len(L1)} persons\r\n- 5    : {len(L5)} persons\r\n- 0    : {len(L0)} persons\r\nTOTAL : {len(LZ)+len(L1)+len(L5)+len(L0)} persons\r\n\r\nOPERATIONS\r\n- Children: inner rails; Adults: outer rails (Z & 0 perimeter) for strong outline.\r\n- Call by tags: Z-01.., 1-01.., 5-01.., 0-01..\r\n- Adjust live by skipping every Nth tag if density too high.\r\n\r\nSAFETY\r\n- Keep clear space at corners; no running; monitor ropes/pegs.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n    ax2.text(0.02, 0.98, txt, va='top', fontsize=12)\r\n    pdf.savefig(fig2, bbox_inches='tight')\r\n    plt.close(fig2)\r\n\r\npdf_path, counts\n",
            "end_time": 1757422115.5988848,
            "final_expression_output": "('/mnt/data/SOU_150_layout_20x17m_OUTLINE_0p6pitch.pdf', (71, 24, 48, 72, 215))",

    "675a43f7-1e2f-425a-804c-348048413211": {
      "id": "675a43f7-1e2f-425a-804c-348048413211",
      "message": {
        "id": "675a43f7-1e2f-425a-804c-348048413211",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",

        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "# Adjust density to target ~150 people:\r\n# - Increase pitch to 0.7m\r\n# - Reduce \"0\" to single outer rail (no inner hole)\r\n# - Keep other rails as-is for clarity\r\n\r\nimport math\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0\r\n\r\nPITCH = 0.7\r\nRAIL_GAP = 0.6\r\n\r\ndef unit(vx, vy):\r\n    n = math.hypot(vx, vy)\r\n    if n == 0:\r\n        return 0.0, 0.0\r\n    return vx/n, vy/n\r\n\r\ndef add_line(points, x1, y1, x2, y2, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1\r\n    ux, uy = unit(dx, dy)\r\n    nx, ny = -uy, ux\r\n    length = math.hypot(dx, dy)\r\n    if length == 0: \r\n        return\r\n    npts = max(1, int(length / pitch) + 1)\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        offset = ( (r - (rails-1)/2.0) * rail_gap )\r\n        for i in range(npts):\r\n            t = min(1.0, i * pitch / length)\r\n            x = x1 + t*dx + offset*nx\r\n            y = y1 + t*dy + offset*ny\r\n            if 0 <= x <= W and 0 <= y <= H:\r\n                points.append((x, y))\r\n\r\ndef add_rect_outline(points, x, y, w, h, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        inset = (r - (rails-1)/2.0)* (rail_gap)\r\n        rx, ry = x + inset, y + inset\r\n        rw, rh = w - 2*inset, h - 2*inset\r\n        if rw <= 0 or rh <= 0: \r\n            continue\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry, rx, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry, rx, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n\r\ndef dedup(points, tol=0.18):\r\n    out = []\r\n    for x, y in points:\r\n        if all((x-x2)**2 + (y-y2)**2 > tol**2 for x2, y2 in out):\r\n            out.append((x, y))\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters (same as before)\r\ngap = 0.7\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 6.0, 6.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.8, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\nnum_w, num_h = 3.2, 4.5\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\nPZ, P1, P5, P0 = [], [], [], []\r\n\r\n# \"走\"\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, zou_x+zou_w-0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, zou_x+zou_w-0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+0.9, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+zou_h-1.0, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.8, zou_y+0.8, zou_x+zou_w-0.8, zou_y+0.8, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nPZ = dedup(PZ)\r\n\r\n# \"1\"\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + 0.3, base_y, one_x + num_w-0.3, base_y, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0 - 0.7, base_y+num_h, one_x + num_w/2.0 + 0.7, base_y+num_h, rails=1)\r\nP1 = dedup(P1)\r\n\r\n# \"5\"\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, five_x+num_w*0.8, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w*0.2, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, rails=2)\r\nP5 = dedup(P5)\r\n\r\n# \"0\" outer only, single rail\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x, base_y, num_w, num_h, rails=1)\r\nP0 = dedup(P0)\r\n\r\nlen(PZ), len(P1), len(P5), len(P0), len(PZ)+len(P1)+len(P5)+len(P0)\n"
          ]
        },

    "ab06d34e-56d3-408b-b776-8a79abdac7d2": {
      "id": "ab06d34e-56d3-408b-b776-8a79abdac7d2",
      "message": {
        "id": "ab06d34e-56d3-408b-b776-8a79abdac7d2",
        "author": {
          "role": "tool",
          "name": "python",
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757422183.498427,
        "update_time": 1757422185.5363648,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "execution_output",
          "text": "(59, 20, 38, 24, 141)"
        },
        "status": "finished_successfully",
        "end_turn": null,
        "weight": 1,
        "metadata": {
          "is_complete": true,
          "aggregate_result": {
            "status": "success",
            "run_id": "54da3406-d89d-4d0c-9b31-b88430061cae",
            "start_time": 1757422183.498427,
            "update_time": 1757422185.5363648,
            "code": "# Adjust density to target ~150 people:\r\n# - Increase pitch to 0.7m\r\n# - Reduce \"0\" to single outer rail (no inner hole)\r\n# - Keep other rails as-is for clarity\r\n\r\nimport math\r\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\r\nfrom matplotlib.backends.backend_pdf import PdfPages\r\n\r\nW, H = 20.0, 17.0\r\n\r\nPITCH = 0.7\r\nRAIL_GAP = 0.6\r\n\r\ndef unit(vx, vy):\r\n    n = math.hypot(vx, vy)\r\n    if n == 0:\r\n        return 0.0, 0.0\r\n    return vx/n, vy/n\r\n\r\ndef add_line(points, x1, y1, x2, y2, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1\r\n    ux, uy = unit(dx, dy)\r\n    nx, ny = -uy, ux\r\n    length = math.hypot(dx, dy)\r\n    if length == 0: \r\n        return\r\n    npts = max(1, int(length / pitch) + 1)\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        offset = ( (r - (rails-1)/2.0) * rail_gap )\r\n        for i in range(npts):\r\n            t = min(1.0, i * pitch / length)\r\n            x = x1 + t*dx + offset*nx\r\n            y = y1 + t*dy + offset*ny\r\n            if 0 <= x <= W and 0 <= y <= H:\r\n                points.append((x, y))\r\n\r\ndef add_rect_outline(points, x, y, w, h, pitch=PITCH, rails=1, rail_gap=RAIL_GAP):\r\n    for r in range(rails):\r\n        inset = (r - (rails-1)/2.0)* (rail_gap)\r\n        rx, ry = x + inset, y + inset\r\n        rw, rh = w - 2*inset, h - 2*inset\r\n        if rw <= 0 or rh <= 0: \r\n            continue\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry+rh, rx+rw, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx+rw, ry, rx, ry, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n        add_line(points, rx, ry, rx, ry+rh, pitch, 1, 0)\r\n\r\ndef dedup(points, tol=0.18):\r\n    out = []\r\n    for x, y in points:\r\n        if all((x-x2)**2 + (y-y2)**2 > tol**2 for x2, y2 in out):\r\n            out.append((x, y))\r\n    return out\r\n\r\n# Layout parameters (same as before)\r\ngap = 0.7\r\nzou_w, zou_h = 6.0, 6.0\r\nzou_x, zou_y = 0.8, (H - zou_h)/2.0\r\nnum_w, num_h = 3.2, 4.5\r\nbase_y = (H - num_h)/2.0\r\none_x  = zou_x + zou_w + gap\r\nfive_x = one_x + num_w + gap\r\nzero_x = five_x + num_w + gap\r\n\r\nPZ, P1, P5, P0 = [], [], [], []\r\n\r\n# \"走\"\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, zou_x+zou_w-0.2, zou_y+zou_h-0.5, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, zou_x+zou_w-0.5, zou_y+zou_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+0.9, zou_x+zou_w/2.0, zou_y+zou_h-1.0, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+0.8, zou_y+0.8, zou_x+zou_w-0.8, zou_y+0.8, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(PZ, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+1.2, zou_x+zou_w-1.1, zou_y+2.2, rails=1)\r\nPZ = dedup(PZ)\r\n\r\n# \"1\"\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y, one_x + num_w/2.0, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + 0.3, base_y, one_x + num_w-0.3, base_y, rails=1)\r\nadd_line(P1, one_x + num_w/2.0 - 0.7, base_y+num_h, one_x + num_w/2.0 + 0.7, base_y+num_h, rails=1)\r\nP1 = dedup(P1)\r\n\r\n# \"5\"\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x, base_y+num_h*0.55, five_x+num_w*0.8, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.55, rails=2)\r\nadd_line(P5, five_x+num_w*0.2, base_y+num_h*0.1, five_x+num_w, base_y+num_h*0.1, rails=2)\r\nP5 = dedup(P5)\r\n\r\n# \"0\" outer only, single rail\r\nadd_rect_outline(P0, zero_x, base_y, num_w, num_h, rails=1)\r\nP0 = dedup(P0)\r\n\r\nlen(PZ), len(P1), len(P5), len(P0), len(PZ)+len(P1)+len(P5)+len(P0)\n",
            "end_time": 1757422185.5363648,
            "final_expression_output": "(59, 20, 38, 24, 141)",

    "26424e59-814e-4e15-b88c-cc0a1977646f": {
      "id": "26424e59-814e-4e15-b88c-cc0a1977646f",
      "message": {
        "id": "26424e59-814e-4e15-b88c-cc0a1977646f",
        "author": {
          "role": "assistant",
          "name": null,
          "metadata": {}
        },
        "create_time": 1757422269.682549,
        "update_time": null,
        "content": {
          "content_type": "text",
          "parts": [
            "わかりました！  \n「Googleマップで計測した座標を入力すると、自動で正確な人文字レイアウト図を再生成」する仕組みを作る流れを整理します。  \nこの仕組みを使うと、校庭の形やサイズが正確に反映されたPDFレイアウトを自動生成できるようになります。\n\n---\n\n## **全体像**\nやりたいことはこうです：\n1. Googleマップで校庭の**四隅の座標（緯度・経度）**を計測\n2. その座標をChatGPTに入力\n3. ChatGPTが座標から**実寸スケールを計算**  \n4. そのスケールを使って「走150」人文字の**正確な配置図PDF**を自動生成\n\n---\n\n## **1. Googleマップで座標を取得する方法**\n### PC版（おすすめ）\n1. [Googleマップ](https://www.google.co.jp/maps/) を開く\n2. 校庭の**左上**を右クリック → **「この場所について」**をクリック\n3. 画面下に「緯度, 経度」が表示 → コピー\n4. 同じ手順で「右上」「右下」「左下」の座標も取得\n\n→ 例：\n```\n左上：32.682345, 130.654321\n右上：32.682378, 130.655210\n右下：32.681780, 130.655244\n左下：32.681745, 130.654355\n```\n\n---\n\n## **2. ChatGPTに渡す座標フォーマット**\n4隅の座標をこの形で入力してください：\n```text\n左上: 32.682345, 130.654321\n右上: 32.682378, 130.655210\n右下: 32.681780, 130.655244\n左下: 32.681745, 130.654355\n```\n\n---\n\n## **3. ChatGPT側でやること**\n座標データから：\n- **横幅（m）**を計算（左上→右上の距離）\n- **縦幅（m）**を計算（左上→左下の距離）\n- 校庭の長方形をそのサイズでモデル化\n- 「走150」の配置をその長方形に自動リサイズしてレイアウト図PDFを再生成\n\n距離は**球面三角法（Haversine公式）**で計算します。  \nこれで、Googleマップの座標精度（誤差±数cm）をそのまま反映できます。\n\n---\n\n## **4. 次のステップ**\nまずは、あなたがGoogleマップで測った**4隅の座標**を送ってください。  \nその座標をもとに：\n- 校庭の実寸を正確に計算\n- 「走150」の人文字レイアウト図を再生成\n- 1mグリッド＋番号付き立ち位置＋必要人数リスト入りPDFを出力\n\n---\n\nもし、座標取得がまだであれば、  \nGoogleマップ上で測る手順をさらに詳しく図解したガイドも作れます。  \n\n座標、もう取得できますか？  \nそれとも先に「座標の取り方マニュアル」を作りますか？"
          ]
        },
